lab_overrides.csv\noverride_rows = []\nwith open('/root/lab_overrides.csv', newline='') as f:\n    reader = csv.DictReader(f)\n    for row in reader:\n        override_rows.append(row)\n\n# Load report_template.json\nwith open('/root/report_template.json') as f:\n    template = json.load(f)\n\n# Step A: Filter in-scope assays\nin_scope_assays = [a for a in all_assays if billing rows, match to assay_id, keep latest effective_month per assay\nactive_billing = [b for b in billing_rows if _as_bool(b.get('is_active'))]\n\nbilling_by_assay = {}\nfor b in active_billing:\n int(str(r.get('active_labs', '0')).strip())\n    if aid not in override_by_assay or rev > override_by_assay[aid][1]:\n        override_by_assay[aid] = (active_labs_val, rev)\n\n# Step E: Build per-assay analysis\nassay_results = []\n\nRUNS_SMALL = 24\nRUNS_BULK = 12\n\nmissing_billing = []\nmissing_carrier = []\n\nfor a in in_scope_assays:\n    aid = str(a.get('assay_id', '')).strip()\n    aname = str(a.get('assay_name', '')).strip()\n\n    # Active labs\n    if aid in override_by_assay:\n        active_labs = int(override_by_assay[aid][0])\n    else:\n        active_labs = tests_small = int(a.get('tests_per_lab_per_run_small'))\n    tests_bulk = int(a.get('tests_per_lab_per_run_bulk'))\n\n    # Annual revenue\n    rev_small = payment_per_run * active_labs * RUNS_SMALL\n    rev_bulk = payment_per_run * active_labs * RUNS_BULK\n\n    # Annual reagent cost\n    reagent_small = reagent_price * active_labs * tests_small * RUNS_SMALL / 1000.0\n    reagent_bulk = reagent_price * active_labs * tests_bulk * RUNS_BULK / 1000.0\n\n    # Annual carrier cost\n    # Assumption: each run requires one shipment per active lab.\n    carrier_small = carrier_cost * active_labs * RUNS_SMALL\n    carrier_bulk = carrier_cost * active_labs * RUNS_BULK\n\n    margin_small = rev_small - reagent_small - carrier_small\n    margin_bulk = rev_bulk - reagent_bulk - carrier_bulk\n    diff = margin_bulk - margin_small\n\n    assay_results.append({\n        'assay_id': aid,\n        'assay_name': aname,\n        'active_labs': active_labs,\n       'annual_reagent_cost_bulk_kit_usd': _money(reagent_bulk),\n        'annual_carrier_cost_small_kit_usd': _money(carrier_small),\n        'annual_carrier_cost_bulk_kit_usd': _money(carrier_bulk),\n        'annual_revenue_small_kit_usd': _money(rev_small),\n        'annual_revenue_bulk_kit_usd': _money(rev_bulk),\n        'annual_margin_small_kit_usd': _money(margin_small),\n        'annual_margin_bulk_kit_usd': _money(margin_bulk),\n        'annual_margin_difference_bulk_minus_small_usd': _money(diff),\n    })\n\nif missing_carrier:\n    raise round(sum(a['annual_margin_small_kit_usd'] for a in assay_results), 2)\ntotal_margin_bulk = round(sum(a['annual_margin_bulk_kit_usd'] for a in assay_results), 2)\ntotal_diff = round(total_margin_bulk - total_margin_small, 2)\nabs_diff = round(abs(total_diff), 2)\n\n# Decision\nif abs_diff < 7000:\n    decision = 'adopt_bulk_kit'\n    justification = f'Absolute margin difference ${abs_diff} is below $7000 threshold; bulk-kit policy is acceptable.'\nelse:\n    decision = 'keep_small_kit'\n    justification = f'Absolute margin difference ${abs_diff} exceeds $7000 threshold; keeping small-kit policy.'\n\n# Build report\nreport = {\n    'metadata': template.get('metadata', {}),\n    'analysis': {\n        'assumptions': {\n            'runs_per_year_small_kit': RUNS_SMALL,\n            'runs_per_year_bulk_kit': RUNS_BULK,\n            'switch_threshold_usd': 7000,\n            'lab_override_rule': 'highest approved revision per assay_id, else default_active_labs',\n            'billing_rule': 'latest active effective_month per assay',\n            'carrier_cost_rule': 'annual_carrier_cost = carrier_cost_usd * active_labs * runs_per_year',\n        },\n        'assays': assay_results,\n        'totals': {\n            'total_annual_margin_small_kit_usd': _money(total_margin_small),\n            'total_annual_margin_bulk_kit_usd': _money(total_margin_bulk),\n            'total_annual_margin_difference_bulk_minus_small_usd': _money(total_diff),\n            'absolute_total_margin_difference_usd': _money(abs_diff),\n        },\n        'recommendation': {\n            'decision': decision,\n            'justification': justification,\n        },\n    },\n}\n\nwith open('/root/reagent_policy_report.json', 'w') as f:\n    json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n# Write summary markdown (4-8 non-empty lines)\nlines = [\n    '# Reagent Policy Summary',\n    f'Total small-kit annual margin: ${_money(total_margin_small)}',\n    f'Total bulk-kit annual margin:
